meta/task agent ---\n# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.\n\nfrom agent.base_agent import AgentSystem\nfrom agent.llm_withtools import chat_with_agent\nimport json\nimport os\n\n\ndef _evaluation_summary(eval_path, limit=12):\n    """Summarize completed generation reports so edits are based on evidence."""\n    if not os.path.isdir(eval_path):\n        return "No readable prior-evaluation directory was found."\n\n    records = []\n    for generation in sorted(os.listdir(eval_path)):\n        generation_dir = os.path.join(eval_path, generation)\n        if not generation.startswith("gen_") or not os.path.isdir(generation_dir):\n            continue\n        metadata = {}\n        try:\n            with open(os.path.join(generation_dir, "metadata.json"), encoding="utf-8") as f:\n                metadata = json.load(f)\n        except (OSError, ValueError):\n            pass\n        # A staged/incomplete evaluation is not comparable to a completed run.\n        if metadata.get("run_full_eval") is False:\n            continue\n        for root, _, files in os.walk(generation_dir):\n            if "report.json" not in files:\n                continue\n            report_path = os.path.join(root, "report.json")\n            try:\n                with open(report_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:\n                    report = json.load(f)\n            except (OSError, ValueError):\n                continue\n            metrics = {\n                key: value for key, value in report.items()\n                if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool)\n                and ("accuracy" in key.lower() or "score" in key.lower())\n            }\n            if not metrics:\n                continue\n            failed = report.get("question_ids_failed", report.get("task_ids_failed", []))\n            records.append({\n                "generation": generation,\n                "parent": metadata.get("parent_genid", "unknown"),\n                "report": os.path.relpath(root, generation_dir),\n                "metrics": metrics,\n                "failed": failed if isinstance(failed, list) else [],\n            })\n\n    if not records:\n        return "No completed evaluation reports with accuracy/score metrics were found."\n\n    lines = ["Completed prior evaluation reports (scores are not guaranteed comparable across report types):"]\n    for record in records[-limit:]:\n        scores = ", ".join(f"{key}={value:.4g}" for key, value in record["metrics"].items())\n        failed = record["failed"]\n        failed_text = f"; failed IDs: {\', \'.join(map(str, failed[:12]))}" if failed else ""\n        lines.append(\n            f"- {record[\'generation\']} (parent {record[\'parent\']}), {record[\'report\']}: "\n            f"{scores}{failed_text}"\n        )\n\n    # Show the observed leader only among reports with the same report path and metric.\n    groups = {}\n    for record in records:\n        for metric, value in record["metrics"].items():\n            groups.setdefault((record["report"], metric), []).append((value, record))\n    leaders = []\n    for (report, metric), candidates in groups.items():\n        value, record = max(candidates, key=lambda item: item[0])\n        leaders.append((report, metric, value, record["generation"]))\n    for report, metric, value, generation in sorted(leaders):\n        lines.append(f"- Best observed for {report} / {metric}: {generation} at {value:.4g}.")\n    return "\\n".join(lines)\n\n\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\n        """\n        A meta agent that recursively self-improves.\n\n        Args:\n            repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation results.\n            iterations_left (int, optional): The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future. Defaults to None.\n        """\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\n        # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna otherwise asks for clarification and never edits.\n        instruction += (\n            f"\\n\\nThe repository\'s README.md describes the goal. Previously generated agents and their evaluation results are at `{eval_path}`. "\n            "Work autonomously: nobody will answer questions, so do not ask for clarification. "\n            "Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply without one when you are done."\n        )\n        instruction += (\n            "\\n\\nUse the evaluation snapshot below as evidence, not as a substitute for inspection. "\n            "Review the strongest completed, comparable generation\'s source patch and its failed-task "\n            "examples/logs, and compare against the baseline or parent. Missing or incomplete evaluations "\n            "are unknown, not zero. Make one focused, generalizable source change motivated by that evidence; "\n            "do not hard-code answers to evaluation tasks. Preserve the task interface and model-call budget, "\n            "avoid edits to `domains/` and generated files/caches, and run a relevant syntax or behavior check.\\n\\n"\n            + _evaluation_summary(eval_path)\n        )\n\n        new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\'all\')\n--- task agent ---\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport http.client\nimport time\n\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\n\n\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\n        self._path = path\n\n    def connect(self):\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\n        s.connect(self._path)\n        self.sock = s\n\n\ndef call_model(prompt):\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\n    last = None\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n            if r.status == 200:\n                return data.get("text", "")\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\'error\')}"\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\n\n\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\n    return "\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\n    parts = [\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input.",\n        "",\n        "Use all demonstration pairs as evidence, not just the most visually salient feature. "\n        "Compare input and output dimensions, colors, objects, and positions in each pair; infer a rule "\n        "that explains every pair, then check the proposed test output against that rule. Distinguish "\n        "objects from background or isolated markers, and check alignment or repetition when relevant. "\n        "Do not assume cells are unchanged unless the examples support it. Make the output dimensions "\n        "consistent with the demonstrated transformation and include every output cell.",\n        "",\n    ]\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\n        parts.append("")\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\n    parts.append("## Test")\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\n    parts.append("")\n    parts.append(\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\n"\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\n    )\n    return "\\n".join(parts)\n\n\ndef _valid(g):\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\n\n\ndef _parse_block(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    try:\n        g = json.loads(s)\n        if _valid(g):\n            return g\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\n    rows = []\n    for line in s.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\n        if not line:\n            continue\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\d\\s,]+", line) else None\n        if not toks:\n            return None\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\n\n\ndef parse_grid(text):\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\[\\s*\\[[\\d\\s,\\[\\]]*\\]\\s*\\]", text)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    return None\n\n\nclass TaskAgent:\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\n        self.model = model\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\n\n    def log(self, msg):\n        try:\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\n                f.write(msg + "\\n\\n")\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    def solve_once(self, inputs):\n        """One direct model response -> one parsed grid (or None)."""\n        prompt = build_prompt(inputs)\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\n        text = call_model(prompt)\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\n        return parse_grid(text)\n\n    def forward(self, inputs):\n        """\n        Args:\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\n        """\n        answers = []\n        for _ in range(2):\n            try:\n                g = self.solve_once(inputs)\n            except Exception as e:\n                self.log(f"Error: {e}")\n                g = None\n ---\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport http.client\nimport time\n\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\n\n\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\n        self._path = path\n\n    def connect(self):\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\n        s.connect(self._path)\n        self.sock = s\n\n\ndef call_model(prompt):\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\n    last = None\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n            if r.status == 200:\n                return data.get("text", "")\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\'error\')}"\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\n\n\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\n    return "\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\n    parts = [\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input.",\n        "",\n        "Use all demonstration pairs as evidence, not just the most visually salient feature. "\n        "Compare input and output dimensions, colors, objects, and positions in each pair; infer a rule "\n        "that explains every pair, then check the proposed test output against that rule. Distinguish "\n        "objects from background or isolated markers, and check alignment or repetition when relevant. "\n        "Do not assume cells are unchanged unless the examples support it. Make the output dimensions "\n        "consistent with the demonstrated transformation and include every output cell.",\n        "",\n    ]\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\n        parts.append("")\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\n    parts.append("## Test")\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\n    parts.append("")\n    parts.append(\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\n"\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\n    )\n    return "\\n".join(parts)\n\n\ndef _valid(g):\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\n\n\ndef _parse_block(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    try:\n        g = json.loads(s)\n        if _valid(g):\n            return g\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\n    rows = []\n    for line in s.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\n        if not line:\n            continue\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\d\\s,]+", line) else None\n        if not toks:\n            return None\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\n\n\ndef parse_grid(text):\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\[\\s*\\[[\\d\\s,\\[\\]]*\\]\\s*\\]", text)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    return None\n\n\nclass TaskAgent:\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\n        self.model = model\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\n\n    def log(self, msg):\n        try:\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\n                f.write(msg + "\\n\\n")\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    def solve_once(self, inputs):\n        """One direct model response -> one parsed grid (or None)."""\n        prompt = build_prompt(inputs)\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\n        text = call_model(prompt)\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\n        return parse_grid(text)\n\n    def forward(self, inputs):\n        """\n        Args:\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\n        """\n        answers = []\n        for _ in range(2):\n            try:\n                g = self.solve_once(inputs)\n            except Exception as e:\n                self.log(f"Error: {e}")\n                g = None\n            if g is not None:\n                answers.append(g)\n        return answers, []"\n    }\n    </json>'
